# Trials

The log of Plan 31's exploratory trials (ruling P31-T, CLAUDE.md § 5 "Trials"): one row per boot of `testing/agent/drivers/x_trial.py`, appended by `python tools/trial_score.py <run> --seen "..."`. A trial is not evidence: no row, page or brief cites a line of this table; its run folder stays in `testing/runs/`. The columns: the trial's run id; the variant (`Survive(control=...)`) and the switches it set; the commit the snapshot was made from (`-dirty-<hash>` for a working tree); the fixture; the loop's minutes; kills by the `zombie_dead` events; hits as the frames' reaction runs and the union with the reflex's `hit` steps; hits per kill (the union over the kills; `-` with no kill); the body's error at the presses, its median and 90th percentile in degrees (Plan 32 Task 4: `me.bodyAngle` against the bearing to the press's target at the frame before each press, `grades.x2`'s `facing_body`; `-` with no press read; every row re-scored under this named change, a row whose run folder is gone given `-`); the largest `head_ticks` of the finish's head step (Plan 33 Task 4: the head ticks a press waited or a head_stall handback held, off the melee leaves, `grades.x3`'s `head_ticks`; `-` with no leaf carrying it, every run before Plan 33 Task 1's Lua; every row re-scored under this named change); the minutes fleeing or retreating; the minutes moving toward a named target; the wounds dressed within one game minute of their first bleeding frame over the wounds taken; the melees superseded before their swing over the melees sent; the end (`death@<game minute>`, `cap`, `crash`, `end`); the health at the end; three lines of what was seen, every wound traced to the press or leg before it.

| trial | variant | commit | fixture | min | kills | hits (runs / union) | hits per kill | body med | body p90 | head_ticks max | care@3+ | contact s | engagements | min fleeing | min toward target | dressed <1 min | melees superseded | end | health | seen |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

end with a standing zombie inside KEEP_R re-dispatches the melee, never the ladder's leg. |

## Round two ranking (2026-11-06)

Every row above was re-scored on 2026-11-06 after round two under the scorecard at `0e4c9ec3` (the readings clipped at the loop's stop, a wound every part newly wounded and dressed when bandaged within a game minute, the hits split by reaction type with the reflex's steps and the EndDeath run one landed attack), the `seen` text kept; the per-row before and after is in Plan 31's ledger (`task-15-pre-report.md`). The ranking below was written before the rescore and is not recomputed; the close report carries the re-scored table.

Round two's twelve rows above (`x_trial-*-20261106-*`): the tactician as the control, the two compositions and the ladder as a reference, one arena lone, one arena pair and one street trial each, summed per arm. These rows were scored with the stop clip (Task 14's pre-step). Round one's rows stand as committed, scored before that fix; the controller re-scores every row once before Harden. A trial is not evidence (P31-T).

| arm | kills (lone, pair, street) | hits (runs / union) | hits per kill | min fleeing (away) | min toward target | dressed <1 min | melees superseded before press / sent (in all) | ends (health) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| tactician (control) | 11 (4, 7, 0) | 1 / 1 | 0.09 | 0.0 (away 0.6: 0.0, 0.0, 0.6) | 14.4 (2.1, 9.4, 2.9) | 0 / 7 | 5 / 23 (11) | cap, cap, death@597 (100, 100, 0) |
| mix_1 | 12 (4, 8, 0) | 2 / 2 | 0.17 | 0.5 (away 1.1: 0.0, 0.1, 1.0) | 20.5 (5.9, 11.5, 3.1) | 0 / 5 | 5 / 32 (17) | cap, cap, death@601 (100, 100, 0) |
| mix_2 | 12 (5, 7, 0) | 4 / 4 | 0.33 | 0.0 (away 0.2: 0.0, 0.0, 0.2) | 11.8 (4.4, 5.5, 1.9) | 0 / 6 | 5 / 27 (7) | cap, cap, death@596 (100, 100, 0) |
| ladder (L, Dress on) | 17 (5, 8, 4) | 2 / 2 | 0.12 | 26.7 (away 18.3: 0.2, 3.3, 14.8) | 25.4 (0.1, 0.2, 25.1) | 1 / 2 | 1 / 19 (1) | cap, cap, cap (100, 94.5, 16.2) |

Best by kills: the ladder, 17 (spread 4 to 8 a trial). Best by hits per kill: the tactician, 0.09, with mix_1 0.17, mix_2 0.33 and the ladder 0.12. The per-trial spreads:
- kills: tactician 0 to 7, mix_1 0 to 8, mix_2 0 to 7, ladder 4 to 8;
- hits per kill: tactician 0.00 to 0.00 over its two arena trials, mix_1 the same, mix_2 the same, ladder 0.00 to 0.25. Each street death had hits and no kills, so its hits per kill is undefined.

The gap between the two mixes and the control does not exceed either's own spread:
- kills: 12 and 12 against 11, a gap of 1 against spreads of 7 and 8;
- hits: every hit of the three arms came in its street trial (all six of their arena trials read 0 hits), so their hits-per-kill order is the order of their street deaths' hit runs (1, 2, 4).

Per trial the arena kills differ by at most one between the three:
- lone: 4, 4, 5;
- pair: 7, 8, 7.

In each, all three killed every wave zombie the frames listed, except one listed 22 s before the cap that the tactician and mix_2 each left standing in their pair trials. The ladder's pair trial left wave 1's 10136 and four natural walkers. The arena kills measure the wave delivery as much as the arm: the rows' instrument lines give `server_spawns` against the listed ids.

All three street trials of the tactician and the mixes ended in a death within 48 s, with no step off the square once the group closed. Each read the same pattern:
- the first goal LootBuilding 3 was failed or refused (nothing to loot, or looted already);
- a walk south (the model's ExploreToward, or mix_2's order piece while the goal was pending) took it into the street group;
- the swing, hit and handback re-pins then switched targets every 0.4 to 3.5 s, with 5 melees in each superseded before a press.

The ladder's street trial lived to the cap on 16.2 health through 22.6 game minutes of flee legs, with two undressed scratches.
